17. appōlō-15

appōlō-15 viṇveḷip payaṇam maṉitaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa 43 āvatu payaṇam.[1] amerikkāvi maṭṭilum nōkkiṉāl aḵtu 25āvatu payaṇamāka amikiṉṟatu. ampulikku arukil maṉitaṉ ceṉṟa payaṇaṅkaḷi maṭṭilum kaṇakkiṭṭāl 7 āvatu payaṇamākum. ampuliyil maṉitaṉ iṟaṅkiya payaṇamākak karutiṉāl 4āvatu payaṇamāka amikiṉṟatu.[2] intap payaṇattil maṉitaṉ ōr apāyakaramāṉa iṭttil iṟaṅka vēṇṭum. ampuliyil mūṉṟu puṟattil 'appiṉiṉ' eṉṟa malikaḷāl cūḻappeṟṟa oru paḷḷattākkāḷa pakutiyil viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ iṟaṅkutal vēṇṭum. immalikaḷiṉ arukil vaṟaṇṭupōṉa. āṟṟup paṭukiyippōl cumār 96 ki.mī. nīḷattil oru veṟṟāṟu uḷḷatu. iḵtu eppaṭi ēṟpaṭṭiruttal kūṭum eṉpatu aṟiviyalaṟiñarkaḷi malikka vikkiṉṟatu. oruvēḷi ampuliyil erimalikaḷ veṭittu antak kuḻampu ōṭiya āṟāka irukkalām eṉṟu avarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. mūṉṟu puṟam malikaḷum orupuṟam inta āṟum uḷḷa pakutiyil tāḷ viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ iṟaṅki āyvukaḷ naṭattiṉar.

intap payaṇattil aticayamāṉatu ampuliyil maṉitaṉ 'kār' ōṭṭap pōkiṉṟāṉ eṉpatuvē. palli pōṉṟa 'lūṉākōṭ' eṉṟa iraṣya iyantiram iṉṉum ampuliyil uḷḷatu eṉpatiyum, atu pūmiyiliruntē iyakkappeṟṟu āyvukaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpataṉiyum nām aṟivōm. intap payaṇattil appōlō viṇ veḷi vīrarkaḷ 'rōvar' (Rover) eṉṟa kāril pala iṭaṅkaṭkuc ceṉṟu āyvukaḷ naṭattiṉar. oṉṟiraṇṭu nāṭkaḷil ampuliyil atikat tolivu naṭantu ceṉṟu atikamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷip pārkkavum muṭiyātu. appaṭic ceṉṟālum uṭaṟ cōrvu atikamāki āyvukaḷ naṭattuvataṟku uṭalil vaṉmi illātu pōkum.

intap payaṇattil paṅku peṟṟa talimi vimāṉi ṭēviṭ skāṭ, ivar tām 1966 mārc 16ām tētiyaṉṟu viṇveḷiyil nīl ārmsṭrāṅkuṭaṉ iṇippup paṇi naṭattiyavar. jemiṉi-8 kappalil tīvipattu ēṟpaṭṭapōtu mayiriḻiyil uyir tappiya perumakaṉārum ivarē. uyirit turumpāka matittuc ceyalkaḷ āṟṟum tīrar eṉṟu pukaḻ peṟṟavar ivar. ampuliyil iṟaṅkum nilākkūṇṭiṉ vimāṉi jēms irviṉ. tāykkappalil ampuliyic cuṟṟik koṇṭiruntavar ālḵpreṭ em. vōrṭaṉ. ivar tirumaṇa murivu āṉavar.

intap payaṇattil ivarkaḷ cellum tāykkappal 'eṇṭevar' eṉpatu. itiliruntu pirintu ampuliyil iṟaṅkiṉa nilā ūrti ḵpālkkaṉ eṉpatu. ampuliyil ivarkaḷ ivarntu ceṉṟa miṉvici mōṭṭār 'rōvar' ellāc cātaṉaṅkaḷiyum koṇṭiruntatu. itutāṉ ampuliyil kār yukatti ārampittu vittatu. itukāṟum ampuliyil iṟaṅkiṉavarkaḷ atikattolivu ceṉṟu cōtaṉikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷa iyalavilli. ivarkaḷ atikappaṭiyākac ceṉṟa tolivu 990 mīṭṭar tāṉ. ākavē, ki.mī. kaṇakkil ceṉṟu cōtaṉikaḷi naṭattuvataṟku oru kār iṉṟiyamiyātatu. antat tēviyiliruntu piṟantatu tāṉ rōvar eṉṟa kār. ampuliyil cellum ūrtikkup pala ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ tēvi. ūrtiyiṉ aḷavu ciṟitāka iruttal vēṇṭum; ataṉ paḷu. kuṟivāka iruttal vēṇṭum. atikaccumi ēṟṟak kūṭiyatākavum amital vēṇṭum. maṇ pōṉṟa tūcupaṭinta tarimītum, karaṭu muraṭāṉa tariyilum, mēṭṭilum, paḷḷattilum pātukāppākac cellakkūṭiyatāka - iruttal vēṇṭum. inta ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ yāvum aminta rōvarit tayārippataṟkāṉa celavu 120 ilaṭcam ṭālar (oṉpatu kōṭi rūpāy), ampuliyil rōvariṉ carācari vēkam maṇikku eṭṭuk kilō mīṭṭarāka iruntatu. oru kaṭṭattil ataṉ vēkam paṉṉiraṇṭu kilō mīṭṭari eṭṭiyatu. maliccarivil ēṟiya pōtu ataṉ vēkam eṭṭuk kilō mīṭṭarukkum kuṟintatu. ētō oru paṭakic celuttuvatu pōl iruntatāka rōvaric celuttiṉa skāṭ kūṟiṉār.

tiṭṭamiṭṭapaṭi ṭēviṭ skāṭṭum jēms irviṉum eṇṭevar mūlam ampuliyil appiṉiṉ eṉṟa malip pakutiyil iṟaṅkiṉar. ālḵpreṭ em. vōrṭaṉ maṭṭilum taṉiyākat tāykkappalil 73 maṇinēram 109 kilō mīṭṭar uyarattil ampuliyi valam vantukoṇṭiruntār. appōlō - 14 payaṇattil ceṉṟa tāykkappal viṇveḷi vīrar sṭūverṭ rūsā eṉpavar taṉiyāka ampuliyi valam vantanēram 39 maṇi 44 nimiṭam. aṉṟuvari atuvē atikanēramāka iruntatu. vōrṭaṉ ceyal ataṉik kuṟivākki viṭṭatu. ampuliyil mutalil iṟaṅkiṉavar skāṭ. ivar ampuliyil aṭieṭuttu vitta ēḻāvatu maṉitarākiṉṟār. aṭuttu iṟaṅkiya irviṉ eṭṭāvatu maṉitarākiṉṟār. ivarkaḷ mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷ ampuliyil taṅkip palvēṟu āyvukaḷi naṭattiṉar. evvaḷavō aṉupavaṅkaḷ peṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷuṭiya ceyalkaḷ ovvoṉṟiyum tolikkāṭci mūlam pūmiyiliruntōr kaṇṭukaḷittaṉar.

viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ iruvarum āmpulit tariyil taṅkaḷ kaṭamikaḷi muṭittukkoṇṭu mūṉṟām nāḷ tāykkappalukkut tirumpiṉar. ampuli, ūrti ampuliyiṉiṉṟum kiḷampiya kāṭciyip pūmiyiluḷḷōr tolik kāṭci mūlam kaṇṭaṉar. tāṅkaḷ kiḷampuvataṟku muṉṉar ampulik kāril poruttiviṭṭu vanta tolikkāṭci kāmirātāṉ ivarkaḷ kiḷampiṉa kāṭciyi mutal taṭaviyākap paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭiyatu. ampuli ūrtiyiṉ aṭiyiliruntu kiḷampiya puḻutippaṭalam vāṇavēṭikki pōl tolikkāṭciyil terintatu. ūrti kiḷampum pōtu oḷippoṟikaḷ nālāpakkaṅkaḷilum mattāppu pōl corintaṉa. kiḷampiya pattuvināṭi nērattil ampuli ūrti 20 mīṭṭar tolivu ceṉṟuviṭṭatu. ataṉ piṟaku vināṭikku vināṭi vēkamum uyaramum atikarittuk koṇṭēpōyiṉa. eṭṭu nimiṭa nērattil ampuli, ūrti ampuliyi valamvarum pātiyi aṭintu, ampuliyic cuṟṟat toṭaṅkiyatu. appōtu tāykkappal 109 ki. mī. uyarattil ampuliyi vaṭṭamiṭṭuk koṇṭiruntatu. skāṭṭum irviṉum cumār iraṇṭu maṇinēram ampuli ūrtiyic celutti ataṉit tāykkappaluṭaṉ cērum nilikkuk koṇarntaṉar. piṟaku aḵtu am puliyiliruntu 112 kilō mīṭṭar uyarattil tāyk kappaluṭaṉ iṇintatu. mūvarum iraṇṭu nāḷkaḷ ampuliyi valam vanta niliyil pala cōtaṉikaḷi naṭattiṉar.

pūmikkut tirumpum orumaṇi nōttiṟku muṉpu 35 kilō kirām (80 irāttal) eṭiyuḷḷa oru ciṟu ceyaṟkit tuṇikkōḷit tām ampuliyic cuṟṟivarum vaḻiyil viṭṭuvantaṉar. ōr āṇṭiṟku iḵtu ampuliyic cuṟṟivantu koṇṭirukkum. ampuliyiṉ cūḻ niliyip paṟṟiya takavalkaḷip pūmikku aḷittuk koṇṭirukkum. mēlum, katiravaṉiṉ veppa āṟṟal, cantiraṉiṉ parumaṉ, pūmiyiṉ kāntacakti ākiyavaṟṟik kaṇṭupiṭikka iḵtu . utavum. itu celuttappeṟṟa oru cila nimiṭaṅkaḷil pūmikkut takavalkaḷi aṉuppat toṭaṅkiyatu.

iṉi, appōlō viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ mūvarum ampuliyiṉ īrppu āṟṟaliliruntu viṭupaṭṭu nāṉku laṭcam ki. mī. tūrattik kaṭantu pūmiyi aṭital vēṇṭum. pūmikkut teriyāta ampulip pakutiyil tāykkappal paṟantu koṇṭiruntapoḻutu pūmikkut tirumpuvataṟku ataṉ eṉjiṉ iyakkap peṟṟatu. uṭaṉē ataṉvēkam atikarikkat toṭaṅkiyatu. atu maṇikku 8,230 kilō mīṭṭar vēkattil pūmikkut tirumpi vantukoṇṭiruntatu. 7 maṇinērattil atu pūmiyi vantaṭiya vēṇṭum.

tāykkappal pūmiyiliruntu cumār 23 ilaṭcam ki.mī. tolivilirunta poḻutu ālḵpreṭ vōrṭaṉ tiṭṭamiṭṭapaṭi cumār 20 nimiṭam viṇveḷiyil ulā vantār. itaṟku muṉṉar viṇ veḷiyil iraṣya vīrarkaḷum amerikka vīrarkaḷum naṭantuḷḷaṉar. āṉāl, pūmiyiliruntu ivvaḷavu tolivil viṇveḷiyil yārum naṭantatilli. viṇveḷiyil iṅṅaṉam naṭantavarkaḷil ivar pattāvatu maṉitarākiṉṟār. itaṟku muṉṉar āṟu amerikka viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷum mūṉṟu iraṣya vīrarkaḷum ivvāṟu naṭantuḷḷaṉar. ivaratu viṇveḷi ulāvi oru tolikkāṭcik kāmirā pūmikku añcal ceytatu. ik kāṭciyi vōrṭaṉiṉ peṟṟōr, iru makaḷkaḷ ākiyōr mikka ārvattuṭaṉ kaṇṭukaḷittaṉar.

vōrṭaṉ viṇveḷiyil nuḻiya aṭiyeṭuttu vitta pōtu avaratu itayattuṭippu atikamākiyatu. atuvari nimiṭattukku 70 taṭavikaḷ. tuṭitta avaratu itayam 130 taṭavikaḷ tuṭikkat toṭaṅkiyatu. avari veḷiyēṟṟat tuṇi ceyta jēms irviṉiṉ itayattuṭippu 116 āka iruntatu. kappalukkuḷ amarttirunta ṭēviṭ skāṭṭiṉ itayattuṭippum uyarntē iruntatu. iṅṅaṉam palvēṟu cātaṉikaḷi muṭittukkoṇṭu tirumpiya viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyiṉiṉṟum 1,92,000 ki. mī. vanta piṉṉarttāykkappaliṉ cāḷarattiṉ ōrattil tolik kāṭcik kāmirāvi vittuc cantirakirakaṇam aṅkē eppaṭit terikiṟatu eṉpatip pūmiyiluḷḷōrukkuk kāṇpittaṉar. appōtu ampuliyiṉ viḷimpu ārañcu niṟattil terintatu. pūmiyiṉ vaḷimaṇṭalap pātippu iṉṟi oru kirakaṇatti maṉitaṉ paṭam piṭittatu ituvē mutal taṭaviyākum.

pūmiyiṉ vaḷimaṇṭalattil appōlō 15 tāyk kappal nuḻintapōtu ataṉ vēkam maṇikku 39,590 ki. mī. āka iruntatu. appōtu atu pacipik mākaṭalukkumēl 121 ki.mī. uyarattil iruntatu. vēkamāka vanta ak kappal pūmiyiṉ vaḷimaṇṭalattik kiḻittukkoṇṭu vantatāl ataṉ veḷiveppam 2,204°C āka uyarntatu. atu cenniṟak kōḷamākak kāṭci aḷittatu. ataṉ orupuṟam erintum viṭṭatu. āyiṉ veḷiyil ivvaḷavu veppam iruppiṉum uḷḷē aḵtu 27°C ākavē iruntatu. anniliyilēyē irukkap pātukāppāṉa ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceyyap peṟṟiruntaṉa. intac camayattil cumār mūṉṟu nimiṭa nēram pūmikku entavita ceytit toṭarpum illāmal iruttatu.

mūṉṟu viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷum tiṭṭamiṭṭapaṭi pacipik mākaṭalil vantiṟaṅkiṉar.[3] avarkaḷ kaṭalil iṟaṅkuvataṟku muṉ avarkaḷ irunta kūṇṭu tolikkāṭciyil naṉku terintatu. kūṇṭiṉ mūṉṟu kutikoṭikaḷuḷ oṉṟu cariyāka viriyātatāl, kūṇṭu cāyntu viḻuntatu ; atircciyum atikamāka iruntatu. viḻumpōtu ataṉ vēkam maṇikku 30 ki. mī. irukkum eṉa etirpārttaṉar. āṉāl atu 33 ki. mī. vēkattil viḻuntatu. nalla vēḷiyāka kaṭal amitiyāka iruntatu ; atikak kāṟṟum illi. viriyāta kutikoṭi kaṭalil mūḻkiviṭṭatāl atil ēṟpaṭṭa cīrkēṭṭi aṟutiyiṭa muṭiyavilli.

mīṭpuppaṭaku oṉṟu avarkaḷi aṇmiyilirunta 'okkiṉavā' eṉṟa kappalil koṇṭu cērttatu. kappalil vīrarkaṭku nāṉku maṇinēram maruttuva cōtaṉikaḷ naṭipeṟṟaṉa. mutal cōtaṉikaḷilēyē avarkaḷ nalla uṭal nalattuṭaṉ iruppatu terintatu. ampulikkuc ceṉṟu tirumpiya piṉpu. 'kuvāraṇṭiṉ' etuvumiṉṟi naṭamāṭa aṉumatikkap peṟṟa vīrarkaḷuḷ inta mūvarkaḷē mutalāṉavarkaḷ āvar. ampuliyil mutaṉ mutalākak kārōṭṭiya appōlō - 15 viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷukku mūṉṟu kōṭi kārōṭṭikaḷ tam pārāṭṭiyum makiḻcciyiyum terivittaṉar. amerikka makkaḷ talivar niksaṉ “appōlō - 15 payaṇam pūmiyiṉ varalāṟṟil oruputu yukattit tōṟṟuvikkakkūṭiya aṟiviyal aṟivi aḷikkum. mūṉṟu viṇveḷi vīrarkaṭkum nām talivaṇaṅkukiṉṟōm“ eṉṟu pārāṭṭiṉār.

aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭavaṟṟi intap payaṇattiṉ uṟutip payaṉkaḷāka (rikārṭu cātaṉikaḷāka'}k koḷḷalām:

1. appōlō-15 vīrarkaḷ ampuviyil irunta motta nēram 66 maṇi 55 nimiṭam. iḵtu appōlō-14 vīrarkaḷ irunta 33 maṇi 31 nimiṭa kālattippōl iru maṭaṅkākum.

2. viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampulit tariyil ārāyccikaḷ naṭattiya kālam 18 maṇi 37 nimiṭam; appōlō-14 vīrarkaḷ 9 maṇi 23 nimiṭa nēramē aṅṅaṉam kaḻittaṉar.

3. mutaṉ mutalāka ampulik kāri ōṭṭiyatu intap payaṇattiltāṉ. atu maṇikku 27.8 ki.mī. vītam ōṭṭap peṟṟatu.

4. viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ 171 irāttal ampulik kaṟkaḷiyum maṇṇiyum pūmikkuk koṇarntaṉar. itaṟku muṉṉar appōlō-14 vīrarkaḷ koṇarnta ivaṟṟiṉ eṭi 92.8 irāttalkaḷē.

5. ālḵpreṭ em. vōrṭaṉ eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷip pakuti vīrar 20 nimiṭa nēram paranta vāṉa veḷiyil pūmiyiliruntu 313,600 ki.mī. uyarattil ulāp pōntār. pūmikkup puṟattiluḷḷa cuṟṟu veḷiyil mēṟkoṇṭa ulā ituvē mutalāvatākum.

6. ampuliyiṉ cuṟṟu vaḻiyil atikanēram taṅkiyatu (145 maṇi 15 nimiṭam) ituvē mutal muṟiyākum.



7. vōrṭaṉ intap payaṇattil taṉiyāka 73 maṇinōm ampuliyic cuṟṟiṉār. itukāṟum naṭipeṟṟa ampulip payaṇaṅkaḷil cuṟṟiya nērattil ituvē atikanēram ākum.

8. appōlō ūrtiyiṉiṉṟum oru ceyaṟkit tuṇikkōḷ ampuliyiṉ cuṟṟuvaṭṭattil iyakkiyatu ituvē mutal taṭaviyākum.

intap payaṇattil kaṇṭa cila āyvumuṭivukaḷ ivi:

1. ampuliyiṉ tari 25 ki.mī. āḻattukkuk keṭṭiyāka uḷḷatu eṉpati uṟutiyākkiyatu.

2, ampuliyiṉ mēṭṭup pakutikaḷilum malippakutikaḷilum aṅkuḷḷa paḷḷappakutikaḷiliruppativiṭa alumiṉiyam atikamāka uḷḷatu.

3. ampuliyiṉ muṉpakkattik kāṭṭilum piṉpakkattil keṭṭiyāṉa tari 4 ki,mī. uyaram atikam iruppatu terintatu.

4. pūmiyil uḷḷativiṭa ampuliyil mēl tarikkum aṭittarikkum uḷḷa veppa ēṟṟattāḻvukaḷ atikam iruppatu uṟutiyāyiṟṟu.

5. ampuliyiṉiṉṟum koṇṭuvanta kaṟkaḷiyum maṇṇiyum ārāyvatāl mēlum pala takavalkaḷ terital kūṭum.





↑ iḵtu 1971 cūli 25il toṭaṅki ākasṭu 7il niṟivu peṟṟatu.

↑ appōlō-13 payaṇattil taṭi ēṟpaṭātiruppīṉ iḵtu 5āvatu payaṇamāka amintirukkum.

↑ ākasṭu 9, 1971il

18. appōlō-16

intap payaṇam tiṭṭamiṭṭapaṭi 12 nāṭkaḷ 2 maṇi 36 nimiṭaṅkaḷ 36 vināṭikaḷ naṭipeṟa vēṇṭiya payaṇamākum.[1] appōlō - 16 puṟappaṭṭa piṟaku atu cumār mūṉṟu maṇi nēram pūmiyi valam vanta piṟaku ampuliyi nōkkip puṟappaṭṭatu. atu maṇikku 39,200 ki. mī. vēkattil ceṉṟatu. pūmiyiliruntu atu puṟappaṭṭa pōtu ataṉ vēkam maṇikku 50,000 ki.mī. 36 māṭi uyaramuḷ eṉa cāṭṭarṉ - 5 eṉṟa irākkeṭṭu ataṉic celuttiyatu.

intap payaṇattil kaṭaṟpaṭiyic cērnta kēpṭaṉ jāṉ ṭapilyū yaṅ (vayatu 41} vimāṉappaṭi lepṭiṉeṉṭ kāṉal cāres em. ṭiyūk vayatu 36), kaṭaṟpaṭi lepṭiṉeṉṭ kamāṇṭar tāmas kē māṭṭiṅkili (vayatu 35: paṅku koṇṭaṉar. intap payaṇattiṉ talivar kēpṭaṉ yaṅ kuṭṭik kappalākiya ōriyaṉ ampuliyil iṟaṅkiya piṟaku māṭṭiṅkili tāyk kappalākiya kāsparil[2] iruntār; ampuliyi valamvantu koṇṭē ārāyccikaḷ naṭatti vantār. jāṉ yaṅkum cārlas ṭiyūkkum ampulittariyil iṟaṅki āyvukaḷ naṭattiṉar. ivarkaḷ cantiraṉil iṟaṅkiya oṉpatāvatu, pattāvatu amerikkarkaḷāvar. ivarkaḷuḷ jāṉ yaṅ 1965 mārccu mātam varjil ai kirisam eṉpavaruṭaṉ jemiṉi - 3 viṇveḷik kappalil ceṉṟavar. jemiṉi-10 payaṇattil talimi vimāṉiyāka iruntavar. maṟṟiya iruvarukkum[3] ituvē mutal viṇveḷip payaṇam ākum. āṉāl ampuliyiṉ 'ṭeskarēṭṭas' eṉṟa maliyiṉ mītuḷḷa pīṭa pūmiyil mutaṉmuṟiyāka iṟaṅkiyavarkaḷ eṉṟa vakiyil ivarkaḷatu cātaṉi viṇveḷi āyvu varalāṟṟil oru taḷicciṟappup peṟukiṉṟatu.

intap payaṇattil pala aṟiviyal cōtaṉikaḷ mēṟ koḷḷappeṟṟaṉa. ac cōtaṉikaḷukkāṉa karuvikaḷ viṇveri vīrarkaḷ tirumpiya piṟakum aṅkēyē viṭṭuviṭap peṟṟaṉa. avi terivikkum ceytikaḷ vāṉoli alikaḷ mūlam pūmikku vantu koṇṭirukkum. cila cōtaṉikaḷ appōlō - 11 muyaṟciyōṭu toṭaṅkiyaviyē. ippayāṇattil ampulit tariyil naṭattappeṟṟa cōtaṉikaḷ varumāṟu :

1. puvisi ; iḵtu ampuli alṭrāvayalaṭ kāmirā! spekṭrō kirāḵp (yuvisi) pukippaṭak karuviyum toli tōkkiyum iṇintatu. viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ mutalil pūmiyip paṭam piṭippar. pūmiyiṉ mītuḷḷa vaḷi maṇṭalatti ārāya iḵtu utavum. aṭuttu, ampulit tariyiṉ toṭuvāṉatti nōkkip paṭam eṭuppar. erimali vāyu ētāvatu anta iṭattiliruntāl ippaṭattil tulaṅkum. ampulit tariyiliruntu vāṉiyal paṭaṅkaḷ eṭukkap peṟuvatu ituvē mutal muṟiyākum.

2 veppam paravutal cōtaṉi (ec. eḵp. i): ampulit tariyil iraṇṭu tuḷikaḷi iṭṭu ampuliyiṉ uṭpuṟattiliruntu veppam veḷiyēṟum aḷaviṉi aḷantaṟiyum cōtaṉi itu.

3. iyaṟki nila atirvuc cōtaṉi (pi. es. i): pūmiyil tila atirccikaḷi aḷappataṟku utavum karuvi pōṉṟa oru karuviyiṉāl ampulit tariyil iyaṟkiyāka ēṟpaṭum mikamikac ciṟiya atirvukaḷum ic cōtaṉiyāl aḷantaṟiyap peṟum.

4. ceyaṟki tila atirvuc cōtaṉi (ē. es. . i) ampulit tarikku aṭiyiluḷḷa amippi ārāyvataṟkāka aṅkuc ceyaṟkiyākac cila atirvukaḷ uṇṭupaṇṇap peṟum. anta atirvukaḷiṉ viḷivu oṉṟukkoṉṟu 46 mīṭṭar tolivil vikkappeṟum iraṇṭu uṇarvuk karuvikaḷāl aḷaviṭappeṟum.

5. nilāttari kāttamaṇṭala āyvu {el, es. em.) : eṭuttuc cellakkūṭiya kāntamāṉiyiṉ utaviyāl, pala ilakkukaḷil ampuliyiṉ kānta maṇṭalam aḷantaṟiyap peṟum.

6. katiravak kāṟṟu āyvu : - katiravaṉiṭamiruntu mikamika nuṇṇiya tukaḷkaḷ perumpālum iṭiviṭāmal puṟattē eṟiyap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivi katirava maṇṭalam muḻuvatum paravukiṉṟaṉa. intap paraval niliyittāṉ katiravak kāṟṟu eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ik kāṟṟiṉ vēkam vināṭikkuc cila kilōmīṭṭar tolivākum. viṇveḷi vimāṉikaḷ it tukaḷkaḷip piṭittup pūmikkuk koṇarvar.

7. aṇṭakkatir āyvu : aṇṭakkatirkaḷ (Cosmic rays) eṉpaviyum kiṭṭattaṭṭa oḷiyiṉ vēkattil (vināṭikku 2,97,600 ki.mīṭṭarkaḷ ) pāyum nuṇtukaḷkaḷē perumpālum ivi purōṭṭāṉ, ālḵpāt tukaḷkaḷākum. aṇṭak katirkaḷ katirava maṇṭalattiṟkup puṟattiliruntē varukiṉṟaṉa; āṉāl, avi eṅkiruntu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu viḷaṅkā viyaṉulakap putirākavē uḷḷatu. ivaṟṟi ampulit tariyil kaṇṭaṟiyac cōtaṉikaḷ mēṟkoḷḷap peṟum.

8. ampuliyiṉ nila uṭkūṟu āyvu : viṇveḷi vimāṉikaḷ tāṅkaḷ ceṉṟu iṟaṅkum 'ṭeskarēṭṭas' eṉṟa pakutiyil nila uṭkūṟu eṅṅaṉam uḷatu eṉpataṉi āyntu aṅkuḷḷa maṇ, kal mutaliyavaṟṟil vakikku oṉṟiraṇṭu eṭuttu varuvar.

9. ampuli maṇ āyvu : ampuli maṇṇiṉ iyaṅku iyalpukaḷi ārāyat tariyil amiḻntu tāṉē pativu ceytukoḷḷum karuvi oṉṟiṉip payaṉpaṭuttuvar.

appōlō - 16 viṇveḷik kappaliṉ eṭṭukkāl pūcci vaṭivuḷḷa 'ōriyaṉ' eṉṟa ampuli ūrti mutalil tiṭṭamiṭṭataṟku 5 maṇi 42 nimiṭam tāmatamāka ampuliyiṉ malippakutiyil pātukāppāka iṟaṅkiyatu. inta ūrtiyiḷ mūlamāka maṉitaṉ aintāvatu muṟiyāka ampuliyil kālaṭi vikkiṉṟāṉ. appōlō - 16 kappal 16āvatu muṟiyākac cantiraṉi valam vantu koṇṭirunta poḻutu jāṉ yaṅkum cārlas ṭiyūkkum iṟaṅkat toṭaṅkiṉar. 'ōriyaṉ' ampuliyiṉ mēṟparappit toṭṭatum ataṉ cāmpal niṟat tūcu paṟantatu ampuli ūrtiyil ceṉṟavarkaḷ cōtaṉikaḷi muṭittut tirumpum variyilum 'kāspar' eṉṟa tāykkappal ampulikku 106 kilō mīṭṭar tolivil vaṭṭamiṭṭuk koṇṭiruntatu tāmas māṭṭiṅkili eṉṟa viṇveḷi vīraruṭaṉ.

jāṉ yaṅkum cārlas ṭiyūkkum ampuliyil 71 maṇi tēram taṅkiṉar. inta nērattil mūṉṟu muṟi tam ūrtiyi viṭṭu veḷic ceṉṟu āyvukaḷi naṭattiṉar. avarkaḷ cumār 10 kilō mīṭṭar tūram ampuliyil ceṉṟaṉar. maliyil 250 mutal 300 mīṭṭar tūram ēṟiṉar. maliyaṭivārattil kāṭupōl irunta pāṟikaḷ mītum, maliccarivu mītum avarkaḷ jīp ceṉṟatu; pala taṭavi atu kutittuk kutittuc ceṉṟatu. avarkaḷ ampuli jīppi oṭṭakam pōl ōṭṭic ceṉṟaṉar. karaṭumuraṭāṉa payaṇattiṉ poḻutu carivu aḷakkum karuviyiyum, piḷpuṟa ṭeṇṭariyum, piṉpuṟac cakkarat tiruppum karuviyiyum, iṟutiyil perumpālum ōṭṭuc cātaṉam muḻuvatiyum jīp iḻantu viṭṭatu. oru kaṭṭattil avarkaḷ tam jīppi maṇikku 17 kilō mīṭṭar vēkattil ōṭṭiṉar. jīp payaṇam 27•1 ki.mī, taṭipeṟṟatu.

ampuli vīrarkaḷ ūrtikku veḷiyē 20 maṇi 14 nimiṭam iruntu āyvukaḷ naṭattiṉar, mūṉṟu kaṭṭac cōtaṉikaḷum veṟṟiyuṭaṉ naṭantēṟiṉa. mūṉṟāvatu ampuli ulāviṉ poḻutu vaṭakku rē {North Ray) eṉṟa āḻamāṉa ampulikkuḻi oṉṟik kaṇṭaṉar. ik kuḻi nilā ūrti iṟaṅkiyuḷḷa iṭattiṉiṉṟum 5-1 ki.mī. vaṭa ticiyil uḷḷatu. kuḻiyiṉ viṉimpil vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa kal vaḷiyam uḷḷatu. itukāṟum ampuliyil maṉitaṉ eṭṭip pārttirāta ak kuḻi 341 ki.mī. nīḷaviṭṭamum 180 mīṭṭar āḻamum uḷḷatākum eṉṟu oḷippaṭam mūlam āyntu matippiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. niṉippiṟkum eṭṭāta neṭuṅkālattiṟku mūṉṉar oru viṇkal cantiraṉilirunta ōr erimalik kuḻappil mōtiyatāl intak kuḻi ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu karutappeṟukiṉṟatu. inta iṭattil kiṭanta kaṟkaḷil cila cumār 9 mīṭṭariliruntu 14 mīṭṭar nīṉam uḷḷaviyāka iruntaṉa. ampuliyiṉ tōṟṟattip paṟṟiyum ataṉ pū iyal paṟṟiyum aṟivataṟku inta iṭattil cēkarikkappeṟum kaṟkaḷ peritum utavak kūṭum eṉṟu aṟiviyalaṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.

mēṟkaṇṭa iṭattil tēviyāṉa aḷavu palvēṟu vitak kaṟkaḷic cēkarittuk koṇṭu ampuli vīrarkaḷ taṅkaḷ jīppil 'pukimali' eṉṟa iṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟaṉar. iḵtu erimalik kuḻampu uṟintataṉ kāraṇamāka 400 kōṭi yāṇṭukaṭku muṉ uṇṭāṉa maliyāka irukkum eṉṟu karutap peṟukiṉṟatu.

ampulic cōtaṉikaḷi muṭittuk koṇṭu jāṉ yaṅkum cārlas ṭiyūkkum ampuli ūrtiyil ēṟit tāyk kappali aṭintaṉar. ataṉ piṟaku māṭṭiṅkili iraṇṭu paṇikaḷi niṟivēṟṟiṉār.

mutalāvatu: 7.5 mīṭṭar nīḷamuḷḷa kayiṟṟāl tāyk kappaluṭaṉ piṇikkap peṟṟirunta avar tāyk kappaliṉ piṉpuṟattil kāmirākkaḷ vittirunta pakutikkuc ceṉṟu antak kāmirākkaḷilirunta ḵpilim curuḷkaḷi eṭuttu vantu tāykkappalukkuḷ koṇṭuvantu cērttār. ampuliyiṉ palvēṟu pakutikaḷip paṭam piṭittu vittirunta antac curuḷiṉ nīḷam cumār 16 ki.mī. irukkum. pūmiyiliruntu pārttāl teriyāta ampuliyiṉ piṉpuṟap pakutiyiyum anta ḵpilimil pārkkalām.

iraṇṭāvatu : māṭṭiṅkili - niṟivēṟṟiya aṭutta paṇi itu. cumār āṟu kōṭi nuṇṇaṇukkirumikaḷ (Backtria) aṭaṅkiya ōr uṟiyit tiṟantu ataṉmītu cūriyaṉiṉ katir iyakkam paṭiyumpaṭi kāṇpittār. pakalavaṉiṉ katirvīcciṉāl inta uyirkaḷ evvāṟu pātikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatik kaṇṭaṟivutē itaṉ nōkkam. inta iru paṇikaḷum niṟivēṟa 62 nimiṭaṅkaḷ āyiṉa.

māṭṭiṅkiliyiṉ viṇveḷi ulā tolikkāṭciyil kāṭṭap peṟṟatu. itukāṟum ampuli ūrtiyil ceṉṟa yaṅkum ṭiyūkkum maṭṭilumē kāṭciyaḷittaṉar. māṭṭiṅkili antarattil "nīntiya“ kāṭciyit tolikkāṭciyil kāṇa avar tuṇiviyār (eṭṭut tiṅkaḷ cūl nirampiyavar) talimi niliyattiṟku vantiruntār. viṇveḷik kappaliliruntu māṭṭiṅkili veḷi vantapōtu tolikkāṭciyil mutalil avaratu kālkaḷ terintaṉa. piṉṉar. avaratu uṭal terittatu. vēkamākac ceṉṟu koṇṭirunta tāykkappaluṭaṉ kūṭavē avar talikīḻākap pōyk koṇṭiruppatu terintatu. avarukkup piṉṉāl avarip piṇittirunta kayiṟum, tāykkappaliṉ cāḷaram oṉṟiṉ mūlam avariyē nōkkik koṇṭirunta maṟṟa iru viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷiṉ mukaṅkaḷum tolikkāṭcip paṭattiṉ oru mūliyil terintaṉa.

appōlō-16 iṉ tāyk kappal mūṉṟu viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷuṭaṉ pacipik mākaṭalil hōṉalulu tīvukkut teṟkē 2400 ki. mī. tolivil iṟaṅkiyatu. inta iṭattiliruntu 1. 6 ki. mī. tolivil mīṭpuk kappal kāttiruntatu. tāyk kappal kaṭalil iṟaṅkiya kāṭciyit tolikkāṭciyil kaṇṭukaḷittaṉar pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ.

inta appōlō - 16 iṉ payaṇam 44.5 kōṭi ṭālar (311. 5 kōṭi rūpāy) celavil mēṟkoḷḷap peṟṟatu. payaṇam ceyta nēram 266 maṇi (11 nāḷ - 2 maṇi) payaṇam ceyta tūram 22,25,600 ki. mī. tāykkappal pūmiyic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa vaḷi maṇṭalattiṉ vaḻiyāka vanta pōtu ataṉ vēkam maṇikku 39,699 ki.mī. (24,183 mil). viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyiṉiṉṟum cēkarittuvanta, nilāk kaṟkaḷiṉ eṭi 110 kilō kirām. itaṉuḷ 18kilō eṭiyuḷḷa oru periya pāṟiyum aṭaṅkum.

viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyil viṭṭuvanta 'ōriyaṉ' ampuliyi 343 nāṭkaḷ vaṭṭamiṭṭu varum. iṟutiyil ampuliyil vīḻntu noṟuṅkum. itaṉit tavira ivarkaḷ viṭṭuviṭṭu vanta āyvuk kōḷkaḷ 92 nāṭkaḷ ampuliyi vaṭṭamiṭṭu varum.

inta varicip payaṇaṅkaḷil iṉṉum orē oru payaṇam tāṉ uḷḷatu. atil mutal muṟiyākap putika iyal vallunar oruvar aṉuppi vikkappeṟuvār.





↑ iḵtu 1972 ēpral 16.il toṭaṅki ēpral 28 ik niṟivu peṟṟatu.

↑ tāykkappalukkuk kāspar eṉṟum ampuliyil iṟaṅkum ūrtikku ōriyaṉ eṉṟum peyariṭṭaṉar. 'kāspā' eṉpatu kēlic cittirak katikaḷil aṭikkaṭi varum oru paṭakkatip pāttiram: tirippaṭam, tolikkāṭcikaḷilum tōṉṟum 'kāspā' maṟṟavarkaṭku nalam ceyyum oru kuṟaḷi, 'ōriyaṉ' eṉpatu miruka cīriṭa naṭcattirak kūṭṭam. kirēkkap purāṇak katippaṭi, ōriyaṉ aḻa tam valimiyum paṭitta oru periya vēṭaṉ. iṭuppil ciṅkattōlum mūṉṟu naṭcattiraṅkaḷ uḷḷa kacciyum aṇintavaṉ, taṭiyum vāḷum tarittavaṉ. appōlō - 16 viṇveḷi vimāṉikaḷ ampuli ceṉṟu varum payaṇam muḻuvatilum mirucucīriṭam avarkaṭkut teriyum; atik koṇṭu avarkaḷ tici. kaṇṭu koḷḷalām. (aṭutta pakkam pārkka)

(muṉ pakkat toṭarcci)

appōlō - 16 il mūvarum payaṇam ceykiyil ṭeksṭās mānilattil hūsṭaṉ nakariluḷḷa viṇveḷip payaṇak kaṇkāṇippu niliyattār. vīṇveḷi vimāṉikaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpu. koḷḷuṅkāl 'appōlō-16' eṉṟu aḻippar. nilāvūrti taṉiyākap pirintavuṭaṉ 'kāspar' eṉṟum, 'ōriyaṉ' eṉṟum kūppiṭuvar. ikkāraṇattāl tāṉ ivaṟṟiṟkut taṉippeyarkaḷ iṭappeṟṟaṉa

↑ ivarkaḷuḷ kaṭṭiṅkili eṉpār appōlō - 18. payaṇattil jermaṉ taṭṭa kālammi tākkutaliṉ kāraṇamāka aṉuppap peṟavilli eṉpatum, avarukkup patilāka cuvikārṭ eṉpavar aṉuppap peṟṟār eṉpatum niṉivu kūrat takkavi.





* * *





19. appōlō - 17

appōlō - 17 viṇveḷip payaṇam[1] ulakiṟku 45āvatu viṇveḷip payaṇamākum ; amerikkāviṟku 27 āvatu payaṇamākum. ampuliyic cuṟṟi amerikka viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ceṉṟa oṉpatāvatu payaṇam itu. ampuliyic cuṟṟi vanta payaṇaṅkaḷilē mūṉṟu payaṇaṅkaḷ nīṅkalāka maṟṟellāp payaṇaṅkaḷilum viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyil iṟaṅkiṉar. 1968 ṭicamparil appōlō - 8 viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyi mutaṉ mutalākac cuṟṟiṉar. 1969 cūli mātam appōlō-11 vīrarkaḷ mutaṉmutalāka ampuviyil aṭiyeṭuttu vittaṉar. piṉṉar tikaḻnta appōlō 13 payaṇam nīṅkalāka maṟṟellā appōlōp payaṇaṅkaḷilum viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyil iṟaṅkiṉar. viṇveḷik kalattil ēṟpaṭṭa kaṭumiyāṉa cīrkēṭu kāraṇamāka appōlō -13 viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyil iṟaṅka muṭiyavilli. avarkaḷ ampuliyi valam vantu tirumpi viṭṭaṉar. appōlō-17 payaṇattuṭaṉ maṉitaṉ. aṅku iṟaṅkuvatu āṟāvatu taṭaviyākum. pattu āṇṭukaṭku muṉpiruntu āyattamāṉa amerikka ampulip payaṇat tiṭṭattiṟku muttāyppu vittatupōl aminta payaṇam itu.

ampulip payaṇaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum mika nīṇṭatu inta appōlō - 17 iṉ payaṇam.[2] itil yūjīṉ ē. cerṉāṉ (Eugene A. Cernan) (vayatu 40) rāṉalṭu i. ivāṉs (Ronald E. Evans) (vayatu 39) haricaṉ ec. skimit (Harrison H. Schmitt) (vayatu 37) ākiya mūvarum paṅkēṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷuḷ cerṉāṉ talimi vimāṉi; ivāṉs 'amerikkā' eṉṟa tāykkappaliṉ vimāṉi; skimit 'cālañcar' (Challenger) eṉṟa nilākkalattiṉ vimāṉi. cerṉāṉ 1966 il viṇveḷi vimāṉiyāṉavar. itaṟkumuṉ irumuṟi viṇveḷip payaṇam ceytavar ivar. 1966 cūṉ mātam jemiṉi - 9 viṇveḷik kalattil ceṉṟapōtu kalattiṟku veḷiyil vantu viṇveḷiyil 2 maṇi 10 nimiṭam naṭantu kāṭṭiyavar ; viṇveḷik kalam oṉṟu pūmiyi oru muḻuc cuṟṟu cuṟṟum variyil ākāyattil naṭanta mutal maṉitar. piṉṉar 1969 il mē mātam appōlō 10 viṇveḷik kalattil ampuliyi 31 muṟi valam vantu ampulit, tarikkup patiṉintu kilō mīṭṭar uyaram vari ceṉṟu tirumpiyavar ivar. āka mottam ituvari patiṉoru nāṭkaḷukku mēl viṇveḷiyil payaṇam ceyta vīrarāvār. intap payaṇattil ivāṉs tāykkappalil taṉiyē ampuliyic cuṟṟikkoṇṭu āyvukaḷ naṭattiṉār. itaṟku muṉṉar ivar viṇveḷi ceṉṟatilli. cerṉāṉ, skimit ākiya iruvarum ampulit tariyil iṟaṅki āyvukaḷ taṭattiṉar. ivarkaḷ muṟiyē nilāt tariyil iṟaṅkiya 11-vatu, 12-vatu maṉitarkaḷāvar. skimit oru nila uṭkūṟṟiyal aṟiviyalaṟiñar; ṭākṭar paṭṭam peṟṟavar. ivar oru māṇi (Bachelor). aṟiviyalaṟiñar oruvar, mutaṉ mutalāka ampulikkuc ceṉṟavar ivarē yāvār. pūmiyil pāṟikaḷiyum pāṟikaḷiṉ amippiyum ārāyvatil ivar vallunar, ampuliyiṉ pāṟi āyvukkākavē ivar 1965 il viṇveḷi vimāṉiyākat tērnteṭukkap peṟṟavar.

ituvē iravil puṟappaṭṭa mutaṟ payaṇam ākum. iravil ēṉ puṟappaṭavēṇṭum ? ampuliyil nammi nōkkiyirukkum pakkattil ēṟattāḻa viḷimpiṟkum miyattiṟkum naṭuvil vaṭakiḻakkup pakutiyil ṭāras-liṭrāv (Taurus-Littrow)[3] eṉṟa malippāṅkāṉa iṭattil iṟaṅkavēṇṭum, ataṟkuk keṉṉaṭi muṉiyiliruntu kiḷampiṉāl iravil tāṉ puṟappaṭa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. appoḻutu tāṉ vaḻiyil eri poruḷ celavu kuṟivāka irukkum. iṭiyil kalam cellum vaḻiyic caṟṟu māṟṟit tiruttavēṇṭiya vēliyum kuṟiyum. iṟaṅkum iṭattilum iṟaṅkum nērattil katiravaṉ vacatiyāṉa uyarattiliruntu oḷi vīcum.

tērnteṭukkap peṟṟa 'ṭāras - liṭrāv' eṉṟa ilakkut taṉicciṟappuṭiyatu. itu vēṟupaṭṭa iyaṟkit tōṟṟam uṭiyatu ; nila uṭkūṟṟiyalpaṟṟiya pala vaki amippukaḷ koṇṭatu. iṅkuḷḷa kuṉṟup pakutikaḷi mūṭiyirukkum veḷir niṟap poruḷ ampuli tōṉṟiya kālattiyoṭṭiyatu, eṉa. aṟiviyalaṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. cumār 2,100 mīṭṭar uyaramuḷḷa ampulimaliyiṉ arukilirukkum paḷḷattākkiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷum, maliyiṉ mītum paḷḷattākkukaḷilum cila karumporuḷkaḷum erimalikaḷiṉiṉṟum uruki vaḻintu piṉpu iṟukiyavi eṉṟu karutappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivi ampuliyiṉ - uṭpuṟattil mika āḻattiṉiṉṟum ōraḷavukku aṇmik kālattil veḷippaṭṭaviyāka iruttalvēṇṭum.

katirava maṇṭalam, pirapañcam ivi tōṉṟiya vitam paṟṟi ārāyum aṟiviyalaṟiñarkaṭkup paḻimiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ perumakiḻcci tarum. cumār 450 kōṭi āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu ampuli tōṉṟiyatāka matippiṭukiṉṟaṉar avarkaḷ. vāyu mēkaṅkaḷ iṟuki aḵtu uṇṭāṉatāka oru karuttu nilavukiṉṟatu. ampulip poruḷkaḷiṉ mātirikaḷ appōtirunta nilimipaṟṟit tupput tulakkak kūṭum. erimalik kuḻampu mātirikaḷil kōḷiyal aṟiñarkaṭkup pērararvam uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ pūmiyum ampuliyum uṭpaṭap palvēṟu kōḷkaḷum evvāṟu tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟu āyvukaḷi mēṟkoḷpavarkaḷ. ampuli tōṉṟiya kālam mutal iṉṟuvari aḵtu ettakiya māṟṟaṅkaḷi aṭittatu eṉpati aṟiya ipporuḷkaḷ tuṉi ceyyum.

mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa 'ṭārs-liṭrāv' eṉṟa ilakku 'amitik kaṭal' (Sea of Serenity) eṉṉum ampulip pakutiyi oṭṭi amintuḷḷatu. aṇmiyil naṭipeṟṟa ampuli āyvukaḷiṉ mūlam ippakuti oru 'māskāṉ' (MassConcentration) eṉak kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. aḵtāvatu, ip pakuti mikunta poruṇmic ceṟivu uḷḷatākat teriya vantuḷḷatu. iṅku ulōkat taṉimaṅkaḷ irukkak kūṭum. it taṉimaṅkaḷ ampuliyic cuṟṟivarum ceyaṟkik kalaṅkaḷiṉ vaṭṭap pātiyip pātikkum vakiyil īrppu viciyic celuttupavi eṉṟu karutap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. appōlō - 17 inta ilakkukku mēlē paṟantu ceṉṟapōtu 'māskāṉkaḷ' paṟṟiyum, avi ceyaṟkik kalaṅkaḷ mītu uṇṭākkum viḷivukaḷ paṟṟiyum tuṇukkamāṉa āyvukaḷ naṭattappeṟṟaṉ.

cerṉāṉum skimittum miṉvici mōṭṭār vaṇṭiyil ampulit tariyil mūṉṟu muṟi cuṟṟi vantaṉar. appōtu avarkaḷ ceyta mukkiyamāṉa paṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟu nilāt tariyil āyvu niliyam amittatākum. inta āyvu niliyam palavakiyāṉa karuvit tokutikaḷik koṇṭatu. ik karuvikaḷ tāmākavē iyaṅkupavi ; tammil pativākum takavalkaḷit tāmē neṭu nāṭkaḷukku oliparappip pūmikku aṉuppik koṇṭirukkum. itaṟku - muṉṉarc ceṉṟa aintu appōlō kalaṅkaḷum niṟuvivanta aintu āyvu niliyaṅkaḷum aṟiviyal takavalkaḷi iṉṟum aṉuppik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.



muṉṉiya appōlō kalaṅkaḷ eṭuttuc ceṉṟa karuvikaḷ tavirap putitākap pattukkaruvikaḷip palvēṟu cōtaṉikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷum poruṭṭu appōlō - 17 ampuli maṇṭalattiṟku eṭuttuc ceṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ ēḻu, nilāt tariyil naṭattum ārāyccit tokuppic cārntavi. oṉṟu, pūmi mutaliya viṇkōḷkaḷ ampuliyik kavarntu īrkkum viciyiṉ taṉmiyi ārāyntu kūṟum. maṟṟoṉṟu, ampuliyiṉ uḷḷiruntu ciṟuciṟu aḷavukaḷil tappittu veḷivarum vāyukkaḷiṉ mūlakkūṟukaḷi ārāyavallatu. piṟitoṉṟu, viṇveḷiyiṉiṉṟum vantu ampulit tariyil paṭiyum tūciyiṉ aḷavik kaṇikkavum viṇkaṟkaḷāl tākkuṇṭu peyarntu viḻum nilāttarip poruḷkaḷāl uṇṭākum nila arippiṉi aḷaviṭavum vallatu. iṉṉoṉṟu, ampuliyiṉ putika iyalpukaḷi āyvataṟkāka veṭikuṇṭukaḷi veṭittu ārāyum taṉmiyuṭiyatu.

nilāt tariyiṉ aṭiyil pāṟikaḷ eṉṉa eṉṉa vakiyil aṭuk kaṭukkāka uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu mēṟparappiṉ miṉ vicit taṉmikaḷikkoṇṭu ārāyum karuvi aintāvatu. itaṉ payaṉāka ampuliyiṉ uḷḷē nīr uḷḷatā eṉpatik kaṇṭaṟiyalām. ampuliyiṉ īrppuvici evvāṟu paraviyuḷḷatu eṉpati āṟāvatu karuvi āyum. itaṉi viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ nilā ūrtiyil vittu atil ēṟiccellum iṭaṅkaḷilellām payaṉpaṭutti īrppuvici nilappaṭam variya utavuvar. 'nilā 'niyūṭrāṉ āyvu' eṉṟa ēḻāvatu karuvi nilāttariyil ēṟpaṭum arippukaḷ paṟṟiya ceytikaḷit tayārittut tarum. ampulittariyil iṟaṅkiya iruvarum intak karuvikaḷik kiyāṇṭu āyvukaḷi naṭattiṉar. ivarkaḷ ampuliyil mūṉṟu nāḷ mūṉṟu maṇi oru nimiṭanēram taṅkiyiruntaṉar. iḵtu appōlō - 16 viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ aṅkut taṅkiyirunta nērattiviṭa, nāṉku maṇinēram atikamākum.



"amerikkā“ eṉṟa tāyk kalattil nilaviṉi valam vantukoṇṭirunta ivāṉs maṟṟa mūṉṟu karuvi kaḷiyum kiyāṇṭu āyvukaḷ naṭattiṉār. ivar paṇip pakutiyil uḷḷa inta mūṉṟu āyvukkaruvikaḷiyum oḷippaṭak karuvikaḷiyum iyakki vittār. oru karuvi ampulit tarikku 13 kilō mīṭṭar āḻattiluḷḷa nilimikaḷi "rāṭār alikaḷ“ koṇṭu ārāyum taṉmiyatu. maṟrueru karuvi ampuliyiṉ iruṇṭa maṟupakkattiṉ amippiṉi uṟṟaṟivataṟkāka aṅkuḷḷa mēṟparappiṉ veppa nili iṭattukku iṭam vēṟupaṭuvatik kaṇṭaṟiya vallatu. iṉṉoru āyvukkaruvi viṇveḷikkalam veḷiyiṭum puki mutaliya vāyukkaḷ eppaṭi eppaṭip paravukiṉṟaṉa ? avi ampuliyiṉ arukē evvaḷavu nēram taṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa ? eṉpavaṟṟit terivikkum.

inta nīṇṭa payaṇattil viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ mūvarum tiṭṭamiṭṭapaṭi ellāc cōtaṉikaḷiyum veṟṟikaramāka muṭittaṉar. intap payaṇattiṉ putumikaḷil oṉṟu nilavil iṟaṅkiya viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampulit tariyil kaṇṭa ārañcu niṟap paḷḷattākku ākum. iṅkuḷḷa maṇṇi iruvarum cēkarittaṉar. ittakiya maṇṇi evarum kaṇṭatilli. ampuliyil tōṉṟum erimaliyiṉāl veḷippaṭum kuḻampil ulōkap poruḷkaḷ irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutappeṟukiṉṟatu. ap poruḷkaḷ uyiriyattuṭaṉ (Oxygen) cērntu inta ārañcu niṟa maṇ (Rust) uṇṭākiyirukkavēṇṭum. eṉṟu ūkam ceykiṉṟaṉar. iḵtu uṇmiyāyiṉ ampuliyil nīrum uyiriyamum iruntirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟum, itiliruntu ampulik kaṭalkaḷil 300 kōṭi āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar erimalik kuḻampu nirampiyirunta poḻutu aḵtu uyiraṟṟup pōyviṭavilli eṉpatu terikiṉṟatu eṉṟum karutukiṉṟaṉar.



inta appōlō payaṇattil aintu ciṟiya cuṇṭelikaḷum[4] viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷuṭaṉ payaṇam ceytaṉa. maṉitarum piṟa uyirkaḷum ampulikkup payaṇam ceytatu ituvē mutal taṭaviyākum. viṇveḷiyil aṇṭak katirkaḷ (Cosmicrays) paraviyuḷḷaṉa. avi maṉitarit tākkum poḻutu palvēṟu uṟuppukaḷilum palavaki viḷivukaḷi uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa. ik katirkaḷ mūḷiyil ettakiya viḷivukaḷi uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati aṟiya intac cuṇṭelikaḷ ampuli maṇṭalattiṟku eṭuttuc cellap peṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ mūḷiyil aṇṭak katirkaḷip pativuceyyum karuvikaḷ poruttap peṟṟiruntaṉa. appōlōk kalattil ivāṉs inta elikaḷuṭaṉ ampuliyic cuṟṟik koṇṭiruntār. pūmikkut tirumpiya piṉṉar ivvelikaḷiṉ mūḷiyi ārāyntu atil aṇṭak katirkaḷiṉ viḷivukaḷik kaṇittu aṟivar.



↑ itu ṭicampar 7ām nāḷ (1972) toṭaṅki 19-ām nāḷ niṟivu peṟṟatu.

↑ puṟappaṭṭatiliruntu vantu iṟaṅkum vari āṉa kālam 12nāḷ 16 maṇi 31 nimiṭam ākum. appōlō-15 viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ taṅkiya nēratti viṭa maṇi nēram atikamākum.

↑ turukki nāṭṭiluḷḷa pāras eṉṟa malittoṭariṉ peyariyum 19 āvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta āstiriya nāṭṭu vāṉiyal vallunarum kaṇita mētiyumāṉa viṭrāv eṉpār peyariyum iṇittu iṭappeṟṟatu ip peyar.

↑ viraliṉ nuṉi aḷavu uḷḷavi. ovvoṉṟiṉ eṭiyum 1/3 avuṉs aḷavē yākum, maṉitarkaṭku muṉṉar viṇveḷikkuc ceṉṟa piṟa uyirkaṭkup peyar vittatu pōl ivaṟṟiṟkup peyar vikka villi.





* * *





20. etirkālat tiṭṭaṅkaḷ

aṭutta irupattintu āṇṭukaḷ variyil amerikkā putitāka nilavup payaṇam etiyum mēṟkoḷḷap pōvatilli eṉṟu aṟivittuḷḷatu. iraṣyāvum ampulikku maṉitaṉi aṉuppum ārāycciyi mēṟkoḷvatāka aṟivikkavilli. eṉavē, inta nūṟṟāṇṭil ampuliyil maṉitaṉ ārāycciyum appōlō-17 iṉ payaṇattuṭaṉ muṭivuṟṟatākak koḷḷalām. āyiṉum, ituvari ampuliyil iṟaṅkiya āṟu appōlō kalaṅkaḷum[1] niṟuvi vantuḷḷa āṟu āyvu niliyaṅkaḷum toṭarntu aṟiviyal ceytikaḷi aṉuppikkoṇṭē irukkum. ākavē, iṉṉum nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷukku ampuli āyvu ippuviyil toṭarntu naṭi peṟṟuk koṇṭirukkum. mēlum, ampuliyiṉiṉṟum ippuvikkuk koṇarappeṟṟa poruḷkaḷi āyntu aṟiviyal uṇmikaḷik kāṇum paṇiyum eḷitil muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvataṟkilli. eṉavē ampulip payaṇam niṉṟuviṭṭālum, ampuli paṟṟiya putupputuc ceytikaḷ toṭarntu veḷiyākikkoṇṭē irukkum eṉpati nām etirpārkkalām.

itaṟkiṭiyil amerikkā putumiyāṉa iru perun tiṭṭaṅkaḷi mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷatu. aviyāvaṉa :

(1) viṇveḷi - āyvakam - (sky lab) : - iḵtu āṟu aṟikaḷ koṇṭa oru vīṭu pōṉṟu peritāka irukkum. itu viṇveḷiyil celuttappeṟum.[2] itil maṉitar evarum cellār. āṉāl, maṟu nāḷ appōlō kalam, oṉṟil mūṉṟu viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ paṟantu ceṉṟu pūmiyi valam vantu koṇṭirukkum viṇveḷi āyvuk kūṭattil iṇivar. piṉṉar avarkaḷ āyvakattil nuḻintu aṅku amikkappeṟṟirukkum karuvikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttic cōtaṉikaḷ naṭattuvar. 28 nāṭkaḷ kaḻittut tam kalattiṉuḷ mīṇṭum pukuntu pūmikkut tirumpuvar, viṇveḷi āyvakam kāliyākap pūmiyic cuṟṟik koṇṭirukkum. iraṇṭu tiṅkaṭkup piṉṉar iraṇṭāvatu viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ kuḻu puviyiṉiṉṟum puṟappaṭṭu āyvakatti aṭiyum. aṅku ivarkaḷ 55 nāṭkaḷ taṅki āyvukaḷ naṭattip pūmikkut tirumpuvar. oru māta iṭiveḷikkup piṉṉar mūṉṟāvatu kuḻu ceṉṟu āyvakattil iṉṉoru 56 nāṭkaḷ taṅki āyvukaḷi mēṟkoḷḷum. iḵtu amerikkāviṉ viṇveḷi āyvakam paṟṟiya tiṭṭam ākum.

mēṟkūṟiya ittiṭṭam puvi āyvi miyamākak koṇṭatu. naṭappu āṇṭil (1973) eṭṭut tiṅkaḷ kālam viṇveḷi āyvakam pūmiyic cuṟṟi valamvantu koṇṭirukkum. inta āyvakam tiraṭṭiya paṭaṅkaḷiyum ceytikaḷiyum āyntu pūmipaṟṟiya ciṟappāṉa ceytikaḷit tayārikka intiyā uṭpaṭa 17 nāṭukaḷic cērnta aṟiviyalaṟiñarkaḷi 'nācā' eṉṟa viṇveḷi niliyam tērnteṭuttuḷḷatu. ivarkaḷuṭiya āyvu vēḷāṇmi, kāṭu vaḷarppu, mīṉ piṭippu, cūḻ nilikkēṭu taṭuppu, kaṉivaḷam kāṇṭal, vaṟaṭci, veḷḷat taṭuppu, nilap paṭam varital pōṉṟa palvēṟu tuṟikaḷukkup peritum utavum.

inta āyvakam viṇveḷi āyvukaḷ naṭattavum payaṉpaṭum. pūmiyic cūḻntu kāṟṟu maṇṭalam uḷḷatāl pūmiyiṉiṉṟum viṇveḷi āyvukaḷi mēṟkoḷḷum poḻutu iṭiyilirukkum vaḷimaṇṭalam aṟiviyal uṇmikaḷit taṭuttu viṭukiṉṟatu ; cila camayam tirittum viṭukiṉṟatu. vaḷimaṇṭalattik kaṭantu viṇ veḷiyil cuḻalum āyvakattiliruntu āyvukaḷi mēṟkoḷḷum poḻutu inta iṭiyūṟukaḷ akalum. ātaliṉ, inta muṟiyil putiya ceytikaḷ tulliyamākak kiṭikkum vāyppu uṇṭākiṉṟatu.

(2) viṇveḷi - ōṭam (Space shuttle) : viṇveḷi āyvakat tiṭṭatti aṭutta muyaṟci itupaṟṟiyatu. iḵtu 1978il mēṟkoḷḷap peṟum. viṇveḷiyil puviyic cuṟṟip paṟantu koṇṭirukkum viṇveḷi āyvakam allatu viṇveḷi araṅkiṟkup (Space plateform) pūmiyiliruntu ceṉṟuvarap payaṉpaṭuvatu viṇveḷi ōṭam. itu viṇ veḷikkup paṟantu ceṉṟu, taṉatu paṇi muṭinta piṉṉar, puvikkut tirumpi oru cātāraṇa vimāṉam iṟaṅkuvatu pōṉṟu pātukāppāka vimāṉa taḷattil iṟaṅka vallatu. itē ōṭatti mīṇṭum mīṇṭum 100 muṟi payaṉpaṭuttalām. itaṉāl viṇveḷip payaṇac celavu kuṟikiṉṟatu. 12 payaṇikaḷiyum 4 vimāṉikaḷiyum koṇṭu cella valla itil evarum payaṇam ceyyalām. appōlō kalatti iyakkic cellum viṇveḷi vimāṉikaḷukku irukkavēṇṭiya aḷavu tiṟami inta ōṭatti iyakkum vimāṉikaṭku irukka vēṇṭiyatilli. ivarkaḷuṭiya tiṟam, cōtaṉi vimāṉikaṭkum (Test pilots) viṇveḷi vimāṉikaṭkum (Astronuts) iṭippaṭṭatāka iruntāl pōtum eṉṟu kaṇikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. āyiṉ, ivarkaḷ kaṭaṟpayaṇat tuṟiyil nalla payiṟci peṟṟavarkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum.

kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu cumār 1200 mīṭṭarukkum atikamāṉa uyarattiliruntu viṇveḷi ōṭattic celuttuvatu ukantatāka irukkum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉi viṇveḷikku untic cellum irākkeṭṭukaḷ itaṉ veḷippuṟamāka amikkap peṟṟirukkum. viṇveḷi ōṭam mēlē ceṉṟu pūmiyic cuṟṟat toṭaṅkiyatum inta irākkeṭṭukaḷ tāmākak kaḻaṉṟu pūmiyi vantaṭiyum. ivaṟṟi mīṇṭum payaṉpaṭuttalām. pūmiyiṉ cuṟṟuvaḻiyil ōṭivarum ōṭam aṅku mitantu varum viṇveḷi āyvakattuṭaṉ iṇiyum. appoḻutu ōṭattil uḷḷavarkaḷ āyvakattiṉuḷ nuḻintu koḷvar. piṉṉar inta ōṭam pirintu pūmiyi nōkki vantu vimāṉa taḷattil pātukāppāka iṟaṅkum. inta ōṭatti mitavik kuṭi (Parachute) vāyilākavum kaṭalil iṟaṅkac ceyyalām. ippoḻutu mēṟkoḷḷappeṟum viṇveḷip payaṇattil oru viṇveḷik kalattip pūmiyic cuṟṟi valamvarumāṟu aṉuppa ōr irāttal eṭikkuc cumār 600 mutal 700 ṭālar (oru ṭālar 7 rūpāy) vari celavākiṟa tākak kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷaṉar; āṉāl, viṇveḷi ōṭattiyum ataṉic celuttum irākkeṭṭukaḷiyum {Liquid Rockets) paṉmuṟi payaṉ paṭuttalāmātaliṉ, inta muṟiyil ōr irāttal eṭikkuc cumār 100 ṭālarukkuk kuṟivākavē celavākum eṉak kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. 1980-vari intat tiṭṭaṅkaḷ ceyaṟpaṭṭu varum.

intap pattāṇṭuk kālattil ip puviyōriṉ karuttiṉik kavarum vēṟoru ceytiyum uḷḷatu, aḵtāvatu, 1975 il amerikkāviṉ appōlō kalamum iraṣyāviṉ cōyūs kalamum viṇṇilē iṇiyap pōkiṉṟaṉa. kōṭpāṭukaḷil etirmuṉiyil uḷḷa inta iru nāṭukaḷum viṇveḷit tuṟiyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu aṟiviyal toḻil nuṭpat tuṟikaḷil taṉittaṉiyē kaṇṭuvarum muṉṉēṟṟaṅkaḷiṉ parimāṟṟattiṟku itaṉāl oruvaḻiyum tōṉṟalām. intak kūṭṭu muyaṟciyāl viṇveḷittuṟiyiluḷḷa cila cikkalkaḷum tīrkkappeṟalām. itaṉāl amerikka viṇveḷi vimāṉikaḷ tam takutikaḷuṭaṉ iraṣyamoḻi kaṟkavēṇṭiya niliyum tōṉṟalām.





↑ ivaṟṟuḷ mūṉṟu ampuliyīṉ vaṭapātiyilum mūṉṟu ataṉ teṉpātiyilumāka amintuḷḷaṉa. inta āṟil iraṇṭu mēṟpātiyilum, nāṉku kīḻppātiyilum irukkum.

↑ 1973 - ēpral 30 ām nāḷ.

inta miṉṉūlip paṟṟi


uṅkaḷukku immiṉṉūl, iṇiya nūlakamāṉa, vikkimūlattil iruntu kiṭittuḷḷatu[1].

inta iṇiya nūlakam taṉṉārvalarkaḷāl vaḷarukiṟatu. vikkimūlam patiya taṉṉārvalarkaḷi varavēṟkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum vikkimūlattil iṇintu mēlum pala miṉṉūlkaḷi aṉivarum paṭikkumāṟu ceyyalām.

mikunta akkaṟiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻi ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻikaḷi nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.

ippaṭippākkam, kaṭṭaṟṟa urimaṅkaḷōṭu (potukaḷa /kuṉu -Commons /GNU FDL )[2][3] ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, inta uriyi nīṅkaḷ maṟṟavarōṭu pakiralām; māṟṟi mēmpaṭuttalām; vaṇika nōkkattōṭum, vaṇika nōkkamiṉṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām

immiṉṉūl cāttiyamāvataṟku paṅkaḷittavarkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:

Deepa arul kaniyam

Rabiyathul kaniyam

Arularasan. G

Balajijagadesh

Sridhar G

Rabiyathul

TI Buhari

Info-farmer

Joshua-timothy-J

HoboJones

Fleshgrinder

Be..anyone

Rocket000

Xato

Mecredis
